  13 |     | 
  14 |     | /**
  15 |     |  * @title Forwarder
  16 |     |  * @notice Last hop of the ZeroDev Smart Routing Address (SRA) deposit flow on Base. `forward` pulls USDC from the
  17 |     |  * caller, charges the Luca fee, delivers the rest to the user (as USDC or as shares of an ERC-4626 vault) and then
  18 |     |  * tries to deliver the whole remaining USDC balance of this contract to the treasury (as USDC or as vault shares).
  19 |     |  *
  20 |     |  * @dev Design rules:
  21 |     |  * - `paused()` means "fees are paused": while paused the fee is zero and `forward` keeps working as a pure router.
  22 |     |  *   `forward` is never paused.
  23 |     |  * - If the user vault deposit fails, the user receives USDC directly instead.
  24 |     |  * - Treasury delivery is best effort: if it fails the USDC stays in this contract and the next `forward` retries
  25 |     |  *   with the whole accumulated balance. It never reverts a user delivery.
  26 |     |  * - The vault is a trusted dependency set by the default admin. While the routing mode uses it, the vault holds an
  27 |     |  *   unlimited USDC allowance from this contract.
  28 |     |  * - The contract is not upgradeable.
  29 |     |  */
  30 | *   | contract Forwarder is AccessControlDefaultAdminRules, Pausable, ReentrancyGuardTransient {
  31 |     |     using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
  32 |     |

 189 |     |     /**
 190 |     |      * @notice Pulls `amount` USDC from the caller (allowance required), charges the fee, delivers `amount - fee` to
 191 |     |      * `recipient` and then tries to deliver the whole remaining USDC balance of this contract to the treasury.
 192 |     |      * @dev Reverts if `token` is not USDC, if `recipient` is invalid, if the fee is larger than `amount` (checked
 193 |     |      * arithmetic), if the USDC pull fails or if the direct USDC delivery to the user fails. A failed user vault
 194 |     |      * deposit falls back to a direct USDC transfer. A failed treasury delivery never reverts.
 195 |     |      * @param token Must be USDC. Kept in the signature for compatibility with the SRA call.
 196 |     |      * @param amount USDC amount pulled from the caller.
 197 |     |      * @param recipient Receiver of the user amount (USDC or vault shares).
 198 |     |      */
 199 | *   |     function forward(address token, uint256 amount, address recipient) external nonReentrant {
 200 | *   |         if (token != address(USDC)) revert UnsupportedToken(token);
 201 | *   |         if (recipient == address(0) || recipient == address(this)) revert InvalidRecipient(recipient);
 202 |     | 
 203 | *   |         uint256 fee = calculateFee(amount);
 204 | *   |         uint256 userAmount = amount - fee;
 205 |     | 
 206 | *   |         USDC.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), amount);
 207 |     | 
 208 | *   |         RoutingMode mode = routingMode;
 209 | *   |         IERC4626 currentVault = vault;
 210 | *   |         address currentTreasury = treasury;
 211 |     | 
 212 |     |         bool userVaultDeposit;
 213 |     |         uint256 userShares;
 214 | *   |         if (mode == RoutingMode.VAULT_USER_DIRECT_TREASURY || mode == RoutingMode.VAULT_VAULT) {
 215 | *   |             try currentVault.deposit(userAmount, recipient) returns (uint256 shares) {
 216 | *   |                 userVaultDeposit = true;
 217 | *   |                 userShares = shares;
 218 | *   |             } catch (bytes memory reason) {
 219 | *   |                 emit UserVaultDepositFailed(recipient, address(currentVault), userAmount, reason);
 220 |     |             }
 221 |     |         }
 222 | *   |         if (!userVaultDeposit) {
 223 | *   |             USDC.safeTransfer(recipient, userAmount);
 224 |     |         }
 225 | *   |         emit Forwarded(msg.sender, recipient, amount, fee, userAmount, userVaultDeposit, userShares);
 226 |     | 
 227 | *   |         _deliverToTreasury(mode, currentVault, currentTreasury);
 228 |     |     }
 229 |     | 
 230 |     |     /**
 231 |     |      * @notice Fee charged by `forward` for `amount`. Zero while fees are paused.
 232 |     |      * @dev `lowerFeeModel` applies when `amount <= threshold`, `upperFeeModel` otherwise. The selected model is
 233 |     |      * applied to the full `amount`. The percentage part rounds down.
 234 |     |      */
 235 | *   |     function calculateFee(uint256 amount) public view returns (uint256) {
 236 | *   |         if (paused()) return 0;
 237 |     | 
 238 | *   |         FeeModel model = amount <= threshold ? lowerFeeModel : upperFeeModel;
 239 | *   |         if (model == FeeModel.NONE) return 0;
 240 | *   |         if (model == FeeModel.FIXED) return fixedFee;
 241 |     | 
 242 | *   |         uint256 percentFee = amount * variableFee / FEE_DENOMINATOR;
 243 | *   |         if (model == FeeModel.PERCENT) return percentFee;
 244 | *   |         return fixedFee + percentFee;
 245 |     |     }

 354 |     |     // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 355 |     |     // Internal
 356 |     |     // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 357 |     | 
 358 |     |     /**
 359 |     |      * @dev Best-effort delivery of the whole USDC balance to the treasury. Never reverts because of the delivery.
 360 |     |      * The vault deposit uses a low-level call instead of try/catch because try/catch does not catch the decoding of
 361 |     |      * malformed return data; a deposit that does not return a full word is reported as failed. Nothing is accounted
 362 |     |      * here: the next call measures the balance again, so a misreported result cannot double count funds.
 363 |     |      */
 364 | *   |     function _deliverToTreasury(RoutingMode mode, IERC4626 currentVault, address currentTreasury) private {
 365 | *   |         uint256 balance = USDC.balanceOf(address(this));
 366 | *   |         if (balance == 0) return;
 367 |     | 
 368 | *   |         if (mode == RoutingMode.DIRECT_USER_VAULT_TREASURY || mode == RoutingMode.VAULT_VAULT) {

 370 | *   |                 address(currentVault).call(abi.encodeCall(IERC4626.deposit, (balance, currentTreasury)));
 371 | *   |             if (success && returnData.length >= 32) {
 372 | *   |                 uint256 shares = abi.decode(returnData, (uint256));
 373 | *   |                 emit TreasuryVaultDeposited(currentTreasury, address(currentVault), balance, shares);
 374 |     |             } else {
 375 | *   |                 emit TreasuryVaultDepositFailed(currentTreasury, address(currentVault), balance, returnData);
 376 |     |             }
 377 | *   |         } else if (USDC.trySafeTransfer(currentTreasury, balance)) {

 380 | *   |             emit TreasuryTransferFailed(currentTreasury, balance);

 406 |     |     /**
 407 |     |      * @dev Deposits `VAULT_TEST_AMOUNT` USDC pulled from `msg.sender`, redeems every share received and returns to
 408 |     |      * `msg.sender` all the USDC obtained by the round trip. Measures balances instead of trusting return values.
 409 |     |      * Requires shares > 0 and recovered assets > 0; a recovered amount below `VAULT_TEST_AMOUNT` (rounding) is
 410 |     |      * accepted. USDC already held by this contract is never used.
 411 |     |      */
 412 | *   |     function _vaultHealthCheck(IERC4626 candidate) private returns (uint256 shares, uint256 assets) {
 413 | *   |         uint256 usdcBefore = USDC.balanceOf(address(this));
 414 | *   |         USDC.safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, address(this), VAULT_TEST_AMOUNT);
 415 | *   |         USDC.forceApprove(address(candidate), VAULT_TEST_AMOUNT);
 416 |     | 
 417 | *   |         uint256 sharesBefore = candidate.balanceOf(address(this));
 418 | *   |         candidate.deposit(VAULT_TEST_AMOUNT, address(this));
 419 | *   |         shares = candidate.balanceOf(address(this)) - sharesBefore;
 420 | *   |         if (shares == 0) revert VaultNoSharesMinted(address(candidate));
 421 |     | 
 422 | *   |         uint256 usdcBeforeRedeem = USDC.balanceOf(address(this));
 423 | *   |         candidate.redeem(shares, address(this), address(this));
 424 | *   |         assets = USDC.balanceOf(address(this)) - usdcBeforeRedeem;
 425 | *   |         if (assets == 0) revert VaultNoAssetsReturned(address(candidate));
 426 |     | 
 427 | *   |         USDC.safeTransfer(msg.sender, USDC.balanceOf(address(this)) - usdcBefore);
 428 |     |     }

 430 | *   |     function _setRoutingMode(RoutingMode newRoutingMode) private {
 431 | *   |         RoutingMode previousRoutingMode = routingMode;

  1 |     | // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
  2 |     | pragma solidity 0.8.30;
  3 |     | 
  4 |     | import {ERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/ERC20.sol";
  5 |     | 
  6 |     | /**
  7 |     |  * @dev USDC stand-in for unit tests. Mirrors the FiatToken behaviours that matter to the Forwarder:
  8 |     |  * - 6 decimals;
  9 |     |  * - `transferFrom` always decrements the allowance, even when it is `type(uint256).max`;
 10 |     |  * - blacklisted accounts cannot send nor receive (reverts);
 11 |     |  * - the whole token can be paused (reverts).
 12 |     |  * It can also be configured to return `false` (without moving funds) on transfers to a given account, which real
 13 |     |  * USDC never does, to exercise the "returns false" branch of the best-effort treasury delivery.
 14 |     |  */
 15 | *   | contract MockUSDC is ERC20 {
 16 | *   |     mapping(address account => bool) public isBlacklisted;
 17 | *   |     mapping(address account => bool) public returnFalseOnTransferTo;
 18 |     |     bool public paused;
 19 |     | 
 20 |     |     error Blacklisted(address account);
 21 |     |     error TokenPaused();
 22 |     | 
 23 | *   |     constructor() ERC20("USD Coin", "USDC") {}
 24 |     |

 26 | *   |         return 6;
 27 |     |     }
 28 |     | 
 29 | *   |     function mint(address to, uint256 amount) external {
 30 | *   |         _mint(to, amount);
 31 |     |     }
 32 |     | 
 33 | *   |     function setBlacklisted(address account, bool value) external {
 34 | *   |         isBlacklisted[account] = value;
 35 |     |     }
 36 |     | 
 37 | *   |     function setReturnFalseOnTransferTo(address account, bool value) external {
 38 | *   |         returnFalseOnTransferTo[account] = value;
 39 |     |     }
 40 |     | 
 41 |     |     function setPaused(bool value) external {
 42 |     |         paused = value;
 43 |     |     }
 44 |     |

 46 | *   |         if (returnFalseOnTransferTo[to]) return false;
 47 | *   |         return super.transfer(to, value);
 48 |     |     }
 49 |     | 
 50 | *   |     function transferFrom(address from, address to, uint256 value) public override returns (bool) {
 51 | *   |         if (returnFalseOnTransferTo[to]) return false;
 52 | *   |         uint256 currentAllowance = allowance(from, msg.sender);
 53 | *   |         if (currentAllowance < value) revert ERC20InsufficientAllowance(msg.sender, currentAllowance, value);
 54 | *   |         _approve(from, msg.sender, currentAllowance - value, false);
 55 | *   |         _transfer(from, to, value);
 56 | *   |         return true;
 57 |     |     }

 59 | *   |     function approve(address spender, uint256 value) public override returns (bool) {
 60 | *   |         _requireAllowed(msg.sender);

 63 |     |     }

 66 | *   |         _requireAllowed(from);
 67 | *   |         _requireAllowed(to);

 70 |     |     }
